### Socket.IO server implementation serving client files

Source: https://socket.io/docs/v4/server-with-bundlers

A Node.js Socket.IO server configured to serve its own client files. It overrides the `sendFile` function to provide the client JavaScript and map files directly from the package.

```javascript
const { Server } = require("socket.io");  
  
const clientFile = require("./node_modules/socket.io/client-dist/socket.io.min?raw");  
const clientMap = require("./node_modules/socket.io/client-dist/socket.io.min.js.map?raw");  
  
Server.sendFile = (filename, req, res) => {  
  res.end(filename.endsWith(".map") ? clientMap : clientFile);  
};
  
const io = new Server();  
  
io.on("connection", socket => {  
  console.log(`connect ${socket.id}`);  
  
  socket.on("disconnect", (reason) => {  
    console.log(`disconnect ${socket.id} due to ${reason}`);  
  });  
});  
  
io.listen(3000);

```

--------------------------------

### Broadcast Project Updates (JavaScript)

Source: https://socket.io/docs/v2/rooms

Demonstrates a sample use case for sending notifications about a specific entity, like a project. Sockets are joined to project-specific rooms, and updates are emitted to these rooms. Assumes `fetchProjects` and `updateProject` are defined elsewhere.

```javascript
io.on('connection', async (socket) => {
  const projects = await fetchProjects(socket);

  projects.forEach(project => socket.join('project:' + project.id));

  socket.on('update project', async (payload) => {
    const project = await updateProject(payload);
    io.to('project:' + project.id).emit('project updated', project);
  });
});
```

### Accessing the Default Namespace (io.sockets)

Source: https://socket.io/docs/v3/server-api

Illustrates how to access the default namespace ('/') using the `io.sockets` alias. This alias provides a convenient way to emit events to all clients connected to the default namespace, which is equivalent to using `io.of('/').emit()`.

```javascript
io.sockets.emit("hi", "everyone");
// is equivalent to
io.of("/").emit("hi", "everyone");
```

### Origins Configuration

Source: https://socket.io/docs/v2/server-api

Configures the allowed origins for Socket.IO connections.

```APIDOC
## Origins Configuration

### Description
Sets the allowed origins for incoming connections. Defaults to allowing any origin.

### Method
`server.origins([value])`

### Parameters
- **value** (String|String[]) - A string or an array of strings representing allowed origins.
- **fn** (Function) - A function `(origin, callback) => void` to dynamically determine if an origin is allowed.

### Returns
`Server|String` - The server instance or the current origins configuration if no argument is supplied.

### Request Example (String Array)
```javascript
io.origins(['https://foo.example.com:443']);
```

### Request Example (Function)
```javascript
io.origins((origin, callback) => {
  if (origin !== 'https://foo.example.com') {
    return callback('origin not allowed', false);
  }
  callback(null, true);
});
```

### Potential Drawbacks
- If `origin` cannot be determined, it might be `*`.
- This function is called for every request; ensure it's efficient.
- CORS headers are only affected for Socket.IO requests when used with Express; use `cors` for Express CORS configuration.
```

--------------------------------

### Register Event Handlers per File (Node.js)

Source: https://socket.io/docs/v4/server-application-structure

This approach keeps the main entry point clean by delegating event handler registration to separate module files. Event listeners might be less discoverable without strong naming conventions. Dependencies include 'http' and 'socket.io'.

```javascript
const httpServer = require("http").createServer();
const io = require("socket.io")(httpServer);

const registerOrderHandlers = require("./orderHandler");
const registerUserHandlers = require("./userHandler");

const onConnection = (socket) => {
  registerOrderHandlers(io, socket);
  registerUserHandlers(io, socket);
}

io.on("connection", onConnection);

```

```javascript
module.exports = (io, socket) => {
  const createOrder = (payload) => {
    // ...
  }

  const readOrder = (orderId, callback) => {
    // ...
  }

  socket.on("order:create", createOrder);
  socket.on("order:read", readOrder);
}

```

### Server Configuration: allowEIO3

Source: https://socket.io/docs/v4/server-options

Enables compatibility with Socket.IO v2 clients. By default, this is set to `false` for compatibility with v3 clients.

```APIDOC
## Server Configuration: allowEIO3

### Description
Enables compatibility with Socket.IO v2 clients. By default, this is set to `false` for compatibility with v3 clients.

### Method
Initialization Option

### Endpoint
N/A

### Parameters
#### Initialization Options
- **allowEIO3** (boolean) - Optional - Default: `false` - Set to `true` to enable compatibility with Socket.IO v2 clients.

### Request Example
```javascript
const io = new Server(httpServer, {
  allowEIO3: true // false by default
});
```

### Response
N/A
```

### Configure CORS options with Socket.IO

Source: https://socket.io/docs/v3/server-initialization

Forwards options to the 'cors' module to manage Cross-Origin Resource Sharing. Allows specifying origins, headers, and credentials.

```javascript
const io = require("socket.io")(httpServer, {  
  cors: {  
    origin: ["https://example.com", "https://dev.example.com"],
    allowedHeaders: ["my-custom-header"],
    credentials: true  
  }
});  
```

### server.in(room)

Source: https://socket.io/docs/v4/server-api

Creates a Namespace object for a given room. This is a synonym for server.to(room) and is useful for targeting messages or actions to specific groups of clients.

```APIDOC
## POST /websites/socket_io/in

### Description
Creates a Namespace object for a given room. This is a synonym for `server.to(room)` and is useful for targeting messages or actions to specific groups of clients, such as disconnecting all clients in a particular room.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/websites/socket_io/in`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **room** (string) - Required - The name of the room.

### Request Example
```javascript
// Disconnect all clients in the "room-101" room
io.in("room-101").disconnectSockets();
```

### Response
This method does not return a response body directly, but performs an action on the targeted sockets.
```

--------------------------------

### Client Initialization - Custom Namespace

Source: https://socket.io/docs/v2/client-initialization

Initializes the Socket.IO client connecting to a specific namespace, either on the same domain or a different one.

```APIDOC
## POST /websites/socket_io

### Description
Initializes the Socket.IO client connecting to a specific namespace, either on the same domain or a different one.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
/

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **namespace** (string) - Required - The namespace to connect to (e.g., '/admin').

#### Query Parameters
- **url** (string) - Optional - The URL of the Socket.IO server (required if connecting cross-domain).
- **path** (string) - Optional - The path to the Socket.IO server, defaults to '/socket.io'.
- **reconnection** (boolean) - Optional - Whether to attempt reconnection, defaults to true.
- **reconnectionAttempts** (number) - Optional - Maximum number of reconnection attempts, defaults to Infinity.
- **reconnectionDelay** (number) - Optional - Initial delay between reconnection attempts in milliseconds, defaults to 1000.
- **reconnectionDelayMax** (number) - Optional - Maximum delay between reconnection attempts in milliseconds, defaults to 5000.
- **randomizationFactor** (number) - Optional - Factor for randomizing reconnection delay, defaults to 0.5.
- **timeout** (number) - Optional - Connection timeout in milliseconds, defaults to 20000.
- **autoConnect** (boolean) - Optional - Whether to automatically connect, defaults to true.
- **query** (object) - Optional - Custom query parameters to send with the connection.
- **upgrade** (boolean) - Optional - Whether to attempt upgrading the transport, defaults to true.
- **forceJSONP** (boolean) - Optional - Force using JSONP transport, defaults to false.
- **jsonp** (boolean) - Optional - Enable JSONP transport, defaults to true.
- **forceBase64** (boolean) - Optional - Force using Base64 encoding for data, defaults to false.
- **enablesXDR** (boolean) - Optional - Enable XDR for cross-domain requests, defaults to false.
- **timestampRequests** (boolean) - Optional - Append a timestamp to requests to prevent caching, defaults to true.
- **timestampParam** (string) - Optional - Parameter name for the timestamp, defaults to 't'.
- **policyPort** (number) - Optional - Port for Flash policy file requests, defaults to 843.
- **transports** (array of strings) - Optional - List of allowed transports, defaults to ['polling', 'websocket'].
- **transportOptions** (object) - Optional - Options for specific transports.
- **rememberUpgrade** (boolean) - Optional - Whether to remember the upgrade transport for subsequent connections, defaults to false.
- **onlyBinaryUpgrades** (boolean) - Optional - Whether to only upgrade to binary-supporting transports, defaults to false.
- **requestTimeout** (number) - Optional - Timeout for individual requests in milliseconds, defaults to 0.
- **protocols** (array of strings) - Optional - List of protocols to support.
- **agent** (boolean or object) - Optional - Node.js http agent options.
- **pfx** (string or buffer) - Optional - Node.js SSL PFX certificate.
- **key** (string or buffer) - Optional - Node.js SSL key.
- **passphrase** (string) - Optional - Node.js SSL passphrase.
- **cert** (string or buffer) - Optional - Node.js SSL certificate.
- **ca** (string or buffer) - Optional - Node.js SSL CA certificate.
- **ciphers** (array of strings) - Optional - Node.js SSL ciphers.
- **rejectUnauthorized** (boolean) - Optional - Node.js SSL reject unauthorized option, defaults to true.
- **perMessageDeflate** (boolean or object) - Optional - Node.js WebSocket per-message deflate option.
- **forceNode** (boolean) - Optional - Force Node.js transport, defaults to false.
- **localAddress** (string) - Optional - Node.js local address.
- **extraHeaders** (object) - Optional - Additional headers for Node.js requests.

### Request Example
```javascript
// Same origin version
const socket = io('/admin');

// Cross origin version
const socket = io('https://server-domain.com/admin');
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **socket** (object) - The connected Socket.IO client instance.
```

--------------------------------

### Registering a Prepending Catch-All Listener with socket.prependAny (JavaScript)

Source: https://socket.io/docs/v3/server-api

Registers a callback function at the beginning of the catch-all listeners array. This ensures that this listener is executed before any other previously registered catch-all listeners.

```javascript
socket.prependAny((event, ...args) => {  
  console.log(`got ${event}`);  
});  
```

### Node.js Server Configuration for Socket.IO Tests

Source: https://socket.io/docs/v4/engine-io-protocol

This JavaScript code configures an Engine.IO server for testing Socket.IO compliance. It sets up listening on port 3000 with specific interval, timeout, payload size, and CORS settings. It also handles incoming data by echoing it back to the client.

```javascript
import { listen } from "engine.io";  
  
const server = listen(3000, {  
  pingInterval: 300,  
  pingTimeout: 200,  
  maxPayload: 1e6,  
  cors: {  
    origin: "*"  
  }  
});  
  
server.on("connection", socket => {  
  socket.on("data", (...args) => {  
    socket.send(...args);  
  });  
});  

```

### Configure Socket.IO with HTTP Compression Settings

Source: https://socket.io/docs/v3/server-initialization

This snippet illustrates how to configure HTTP compression for the long-polling transport using the 'httpCompression' option. Similar to per-message deflate, it allows setting a threshold and other compression-related parameters.

```javascript
const io = require("socket.io")(httpServer, {
  httpCompression: {
    threshold: 2048,

    chunkSize: 8 * 1024,
    windowBits: 14,
    memLevel: 7,
  }
});

```
